# IMPERIA COLD WAR — Physical capacity for existing national building grants.
# IMPERIA COLD WAR — Universal SSR focus programme.
COLDWAR_SSR_load_tree = {
	if = {
		limit = { IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes OR = { original_tag = UKR original_tag = BLR original_tag = MLV original_tag = EST original_tag = LAT original_tag = LIT original_tag = GEO original_tag = ARM original_tag = AZE original_tag = KAZ original_tag = UZB original_tag = TRK original_tag = KYR original_tag = TAJ } NOT = { has_focus_tree = COLDWAR_SSR_1970_focus } }
		load_focus_tree = { tree = COLDWAR_SSR_1970_focus keep_completed = yes }
	}
}
COLDWAR_SSR_reward_secretariat = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_ssr_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_ssr = yes
				}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_secretariat_rewarded }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_secretariat_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_SSR_reward_moscow_cadres = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_ssr_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_ssr = yes
				}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_moscow_cadres_rewarded }
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_secretariat_rewarded
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_local_cadres_rewarded }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_moscow_cadres_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_appointment_school = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_ssr_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_ssr = yes
				}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_appointment_school_rewarded }
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_moscow_cadres_rewarded
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_local_cadres_rewarded
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0008 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_appointment_school_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_combine_priority = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_ssr_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_ssr = yes
				}
			}
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_combine_priority_rewarded }
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_economic_accounts_rewarded
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_consumer_priority_rewarded }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.015 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_combine_priority_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_rail_depots = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_ssr_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_ssr = yes
				}
			}
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_rail_depots_rewarded }
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_output_incentives_rewarded
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_quality_contracts_rewarded
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.003 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_rail_depots_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_new_power_rewarded }
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_service_networks_rewarded

			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_military_components = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_ssr_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_ssr = yes
				}
			}
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_military_components_rewarded }
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_defence_commission_rewarded
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dual_use_industry_rewarded }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.012 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_military_components_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }

				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_ssr = yes
				}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_combined_exercises_rewarded }
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_defence_records_rewarded
		}
		add_doctrine_cost_reduction = { name = COLDWAR_SSR_combined_exercises uses = 1 cost_reduction = 0.35 category = land_doctrine }
		army_experience = 25
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_combined_exercises_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_UKR_p4_production = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p4_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = UKR
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p4_public_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.012 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p4_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p4_targeted_rewarded }
			original_tag = UKR
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p4_development_rewarded
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p4_universal_rewarded }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p4_targeted_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_UKR_p0_institution = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p0_institution_rewarded }
			original_tag = UKR
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p0_civic_rewarded
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p0_executive_rewarded
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0006 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p0_institution uses = 1 bonus = 0.25 category = CAT_electronics }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p0_institution_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

			custom_trigger_tooltip = {

				}
			}
			western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p0_archives_rewarded }

				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p0_civic_rewarded
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p0_executive_rewarded

	}

			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p1_public_rewarded }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0012 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p1_market_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_SSR_reward_UKR_p1_production = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p1_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = UKR
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p1_public_rewarded

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_UKR_p1_consumer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p1_consumer_rewarded }
			original_tag = UKR
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p1_market_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p1_consumer_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_SSR_reward_UKR_p1_development = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p1_development_rewarded }
			original_tag = UKR
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p1_production_rewarded
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p1_consumer_rewarded
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p1_development uses = 1 bonus = 0.25 category = CAT_industry }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p1_development_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_UKR_p3_consumer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_social_democrats_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p3_consumer_rewarded }
			original_tag = UKR
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p3_market_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p3_consumer_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_UKR_p21_production = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			nationalist_fascist_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p21_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = UKR
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p21_public_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0015 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UKR_p21_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_ssr_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_ssr = yes
				}
			}
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_aircraft_works_rewarded }
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_aircraft_mandate_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.015 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_special_armour_works = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_ssr_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_ssr = yes
				}
			}
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_armour_works_rewarded }
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_armour_mandate_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.015 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_armour_works_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_special_army_works = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_ssr_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_ssr = yes
				}
			}
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_army_works_rewarded }
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_army_mandate_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.009 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_army_works_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

				is_coastal = yes
				free_building_slots = { building = dockyard size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			any_owned_state = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT is_core_of = ROOT is_coastal = yes }

				is_core_of = ROOT
				is_coastal = yes
				free_building_slots = { building = dockyard size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}

			add_building_construction = { type = dockyard level = 1 instant_build = yes }

			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_ssr_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_ssr = yes
				}
			}
			any_owned_state = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT is_core_of = ROOT is_coastal = yes }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_navy_priority_rewarded }
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_navy_office_rewarded
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_navy_efficiency_rewarded }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_navy_priority_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_special_agriculture_mandate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_ssr_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_ssr = yes
				}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_agriculture_mandate_rewarded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_aircraft_mandate_rewarded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_armour_mandate_rewarded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_artillery_mandate_rewarded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_army_mandate_rewarded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_navy_mandate_rewarded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_industry_mandate_rewarded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_resources_mandate_rewarded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_electronics_mandate_rewarded }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.003 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_agriculture_mandate_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_SSR_reward_special_agriculture_works = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_ssr_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_ssr = yes
				}
			}
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = agriculture_district size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_agriculture_works_rewarded }
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_agriculture_mandate_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.009 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = agriculture_district size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = agriculture_district level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_agriculture_works_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_SSR_reward_special_agriculture_schools = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_ssr_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_ssr = yes
				}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_agriculture_schools_rewarded }
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_agriculture_works_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.002 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_SSR_special_agriculture_schools uses = 2 bonus = 0.5 category = CAT_industry }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_agriculture_schools_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_aircraft_mandate_rewarded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_armour_mandate_rewarded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_artillery_mandate_rewarded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_army_mandate_rewarded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_navy_mandate_rewarded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_agriculture_mandate_rewarded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_industry_mandate_rewarded }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_electronics_mandate_rewarded }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.003 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_resources_mandate_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_SSR_reward_special_resources_works = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_ssr_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_ssr = yes
				}
			}
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_resources_works_rewarded }
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_resources_mandate_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.009 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_resources_works_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_special_resources_left = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_ssr_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_ssr = yes
				}
			}
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				OR = {
					has_resources_amount = { resource = oil amount > 0 }
					has_resources_amount = { resource = aluminium amount > 0 }
					has_resources_amount = { resource = tungsten amount > 0 }
					has_resources_amount = { resource = steel amount > 0 }
					has_resources_amount = { resource = chromium amount > 0 }
				}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_resources_left_rewarded }
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_resources_schools_rewarded
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_resources_right_rewarded }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.002 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_special_resources_left_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_BLR_regional_standards_rewarded }
			original_tag = BLR
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_BLR_regional_orders_rewarded
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_BLR_regional_diversify_rewarded
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0005 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_SSR_BLR_regional_standards uses = 1 bonus = 0.5 category = CAT_industry }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_BLR_regional_standards_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_BLR_p4_production = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_BLR_p4_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = BLR
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_BLR_p4_public_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.012 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_BLR_p4_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_BLR_p1_universal_rewarded }
			original_tag = BLR
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_BLR_p1_development_rewarded
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_BLR_p1_targeted_rewarded }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_BLR_p1_universal_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_MLV_p4_production = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_MLV_p4_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = MLV
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_MLV_p4_public_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.012 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_MLV_p4_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_EST_p4_production = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p4_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = EST
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p4_public_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.012 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p4_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p0_civic_rewarded }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p0_executive_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_SSR_reward_EST_p0_institution = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p0_institution_rewarded }
			original_tag = EST
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p0_civic_rewarded
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p0_executive_rewarded
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0005 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p0_institution_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_SSR_reward_EST_p0_archives = {

			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt

					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes

				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p0_civic_rewarded
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p0_executive_rewarded
			}
		}

		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_EST_p0_consumer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p0_consumer_rewarded }
			original_tag = EST
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p0_market_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p0_consumer_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_social_democrats_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p3_development_rewarded }
			original_tag = EST
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p3_production_rewarded
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p3_consumer_rewarded
			}
		}

		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_EST_p3_development_rewarded

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_LAT_p4_production = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p4_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = LAT
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p4_public_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.012 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p4_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p0_executive_rewarded }
			original_tag = LAT
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p0_root_rewarded
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p0_civic_rewarded }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p0_executive_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_SSR_reward_LAT_p0_institution = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p0_institution_rewarded }
			original_tag = LAT
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p0_civic_rewarded
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p0_executive_rewarded
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0005 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p0_institution_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_SSR_reward_LAT_p0_archives = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p0_archives_rewarded }
			original_tag = LAT
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p0_civic_rewarded
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p0_executive_rewarded
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 4 }
		set_temp_variable = { target_party_index = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_fraction = 0.15 }
		move_party_popularity = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p0_archives_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_LAT_p0_consumer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p0_consumer_rewarded }
			original_tag = LAT
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p0_market_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0006 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p0_consumer_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_SSR_reward_LAT_p0_development = {

		limit = {

				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p0_production_rewarded
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p0_consumer_rewarded

	}
}

		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0008 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p1_production uses = 1 bonus = 0.25 category = CAT_industry }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p1_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_SSR_reward_LAT_p1_consumer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p1_consumer_rewarded }
			original_tag = LAT
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p1_market_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LAT_p1_consumer_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_SSR_reward_LAT_p1_development = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_LIT_p4_production = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LIT_p4_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = LIT
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LIT_p4_public_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.012 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LIT_p4_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_LIT_p0_consumer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LIT_p0_consumer_rewarded }
			original_tag = LIT
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LIT_p0_market_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0009 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LIT_p0_consumer_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_LIT_p3_consumer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_social_democrats_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LIT_p3_consumer_rewarded }
			original_tag = LIT
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LIT_p3_market_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0008 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LIT_p3_consumer_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LIT_p20_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = LIT
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LIT_p20_public_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_LIT_p20_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_GEO_p4_production = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_GEO_p4_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = GEO
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_GEO_p4_public_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.012 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_GEO_p4_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

		set_temp_variable = { target_party_index = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_fraction = 0.15 }

				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_social_democrats_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_GEO_p3_public_rewarded }
			original_tag = GEO
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_GEO_p3_institution_rewarded
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_GEO_p3_market_rewarded }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0009 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_GEO_p3_public_rewarded

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_GEO_p3_consumer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_social_democrats_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_GEO_p3_consumer_rewarded }
			original_tag = GEO
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_GEO_p3_market_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0008 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_GEO_p3_consumer_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_SSR_reward_GEO_p3_development = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_social_democrats_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_GEO_p3_development_rewarded }
			original_tag = GEO
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_GEO_p3_production_rewarded
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_GEO_p3_consumer_rewarded
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0009 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_SSR_GEO_p3_development uses = 1 bonus = 0.25 category = CAT_industry }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_GEO_p3_development_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_ARM_p4_production = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_ARM_p4_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = ARM
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_ARM_p4_public_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.012 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_ARM_p4_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_ARM_p1_development_rewarded }
			original_tag = ARM
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_ARM_p1_production_rewarded
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_ARM_p1_consumer_rewarded
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0007 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_SSR_ARM_p1_development uses = 1 bonus = 0.35 category = CAT_electronics }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_ARM_p1_development_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_ARM_p18_consumer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			neutrality_neutral_social_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_ARM_p18_consumer_rewarded }
			original_tag = ARM
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_ARM_p18_market_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_ARM_p18_consumer_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_ARM_p20_production = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			nationalist_right_wing_populists_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_ARM_p20_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = ARM
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_ARM_p20_public_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_ARM_p20_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_KAZ_p4_production = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_KAZ_p4_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = KAZ
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_KAZ_p4_public_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.012 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_KAZ_p4_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_UZB_p4_production = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UZB_p4_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = UZB
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UZB_p4_public_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.012 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_UZB_p4_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_TRK_p4_production = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TRK_p4_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = TRK
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TRK_p4_public_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.012 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TRK_p4_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_TRK_p14_consumer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			neutrality_neutral_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TRK_p14_consumer_rewarded }
			original_tag = TRK
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TRK_p14_market_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.002 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TRK_p14_consumer_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_KYR_p4_production = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_KYR_p4_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = KYR
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_KYR_p4_public_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.012 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_KYR_p4_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_SSR_reward_KYR_p14_production = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			neutrality_neutral_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_KYR_p14_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = KYR
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_KYR_p14_public_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.003 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
	tooltip = COLDWAR_GRANT_capacity_infrastructure_1_tt
	check_variable = { var = building_level@infrastructure value = 5 compare = less_than_or_equals }
}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_KYR_p14_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TAJ_p4_market_rewarded }
			original_tag = TAJ
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TAJ_p4_institution_rewarded
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TAJ_p4_public_rewarded }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.002 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TAJ_p4_market_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_SSR_reward_TAJ_p4_production = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_requires_independence_tt
				hidden_trigger = {
					COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes
				}
			}
			emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
			any_owned_state = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TAJ_p4_production_rewarded }
			original_tag = TAJ
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TAJ_p4_public_rewarded
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.012 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				free_building_slots = { building = arms_factory size > 0 include_locked = yes }
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TAJ_p4_production_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}

		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TAJ_p12_root_rewarded

	}

				}
			}
			neutrality_neutral_muslim_brotherhood_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TAJ_p12_archives_rewarded }
			original_tag = TAJ
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TAJ_p12_civic_rewarded
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TAJ_p12_executive_rewarded
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 4 }
		set_temp_variable = { target_party_index = 12 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_fraction = 0.15 }
		move_party_popularity = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_TAJ_p12_archives_rewarded
		COLDWAR_SSR_reconcile = yes
	}
}
